# Test Cases: Main Contract Form — 新規登録 / 複製 / 子主契約作成 (G-01-01) — Phase 2 (real API)

> Driven by [test-plan.md](../test-plan.md), unit 2 "Main Contract Form — Create / 複製 / 子主契約". Covers `/contracts/create` in its three modes: 新規登録 (no query), 複製 (`?copyFrom=<id>`), 子主契約作成 (`?copyFrom=<id>&asChild=1`). That means the frame, all three steps, the pickers, validation, the discard dialog, the save paths (`POST /admin/plans`, `POST /admin/plans/{planId}/copy`) and role gating. **Out of scope**: 編集 `/contracts/[id]/edit` (Unit 3); the list's own behaviour (Unit 1); 主契約 詳細 `/contracts/[id]`, which is used here **only** as an entry point (複製 / 子主契約を作成 header buttons) and as a read-back tool after saving; the Y-07 brand 費用 tab (landing only); the store / corporate / terms masters (read-only input).

## ⚠️ Data-safety rule — read before executing

The dev backend is real, **shared**, and has **no seed reset**.

- Every save creates a **new** master named `QA TC G01 <date> <purpose>` (`<date>` = execution date `YYYYMMDD`). Rename copies to this pattern before saving whenever the case allows it.
- Pre-existing masters (seed `MC-0001`…`MC-0010`, other testers' rows) may be **read** and used as a 複製 source only through the copy form — never edited or deleted. This unit's cases copy the run's own fixtures (N1 / N4 / N6 / N9) anyway.
- Brand, store, corporate and terms masters are read-only input. Never edit a brand's 費用 settings to set up a case; read the current values instead.
- Token replays (TC-113, TC-115–TC-118) that unexpectedly **succeed** create a row: delete it straight away and record it in the footer.
- **Ladyroom (`[Ladyroom]`, TC-160–TC-199)**: the 主契約 these cases create are named per the [029 test plan §8](../../../029-equipment-management/tests/test-plan.md) (`QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-…`, table "Ladyroom fixtures" below), **not** `QA TC G01 <date>`. Tick areas only on these QA 主契約; never add or remove areas on a pre-existing 主契約, and never rename, re-condition or delete a pre-existing area.

These are used per the GUI checklist's limit precedence, not as a behaviour oracle.

- **Checklists**: `tests/checklists/common-function-checklist.md` (Add New, Clone / Duplicate, Single File Upload, Unsaved Changes Guard, Deliberately Removed, Other Cases, Real-API Integrated Screen, and the **new** "Multi-Step (Stepper) Form" section added for this unit); `tests/checklists/common-gui-validate-checklist.md` (Default field constraints, Text Box, Text Area, Numeric, Select Box, Checkbox, Toggle, Datetime, Button, Output Escaping, General Layout).
- **Integration round**: form route is on **Round 1, real API-087**; authored against `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`.
- **No `checklists/` folder** exists for this feature, so the cases come from spec + requirement doc + prototype only.

- **Divergence from the test plan**: the plan's stress bullets assumed 255-char names (common default). The contract states `name` maxLength **100**, which takes precedence, so every long-name case here uses 100.
- **Revision 2026-11-19 (ladyroom)**: TC-160–TC-199 added (`[Ladyroom]`, test-plan LR1–LR3) and TC-041 reset. Sources: `.cache/fitness-spec/crm/requirements/G-01.md` `261112_v12` FR-M002 「利用できるエリア」 + 詳細設定 「性別制限」, FR-M004 (+ #247), FR-M005, FR-M007; `E-02.md` `261109_v6` FR-008 / FR-016 (`fitness-spec@9e0e07e`); spec.md FR-022 + "Ladyroom slice — 利用できるエリア"; `specs/029-equipment-management/009-area-master/spec.md` (area facts); prototype `fitness-crm-ui@9428dc2` `contract-form.tsx`, `components/available-areas.tsx`, `data/facility-areas.ts`; API `src/lib/openapi.real.json` `CreatePlanDto` / `UpdatePlanDto` / `CopyPlanDto.overrides` `areaIds`, `PlanDetailDto.areas` / `genderCondition` / `limitOfSex`, `GET /admin/areas?brandGroup=`; fixtures, pre-flight gaps and run order from the 029 test plan §7e–§9. The edit-mode cases (Category I `編集`, TC-190–TC-198) are here because the Unit 3 doc `[G-01][Main Contract Edit]` is not authored yet — move them there when it is. Divergence from spec.md / the plan: the card is on **step 2** (料金・条件) and its read-out on **step 3** (確認), per the prototype (A8).

**Ladyroom order** (inside the 029 plan §9 step 8, after unit E-02-G created the areas): TC-160–TC-173 (nothing saved) → TC-174–TC-179 (fixtures) → TC-180–TC-189 → 編集 TC-190–TC-198 → the Detail doc's TC-135–TC-146 → TC-199 (throw-aways now; L1–L4 at 029 step 13).

## Priority definitions

| Priority | Meaning                                                                      |
| -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| High     | Core/main-flow function cases, and UI issues immediately visible to the user |
| Medium   | Validation cases                                                             |
| Low      | Rare/edge cases                                                              |

## Common preconditions

Password for all accounts: `Password@123` (`src/lib/auth/demo-users.ts`). The header's 「デモ: ユーザー切替」 switcher appears only with `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=development`. Run the app on `localhost:3000` (backend CORS). One account = one session: use different accounts for two-tab cases.

| ID   | Precondition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PC-A | Signed in as **Headquarter** (`lodu4289+headquater@gmail.com`); viewport 1024×768 unless stated.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| PC-B | Signed in as **System** (`lodu4289+1@gmail.com`), in a second browser profile when used alongside PC-A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| PC-C | Signed in as **Manager** (`lodu4289+manager@gmail.com`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| PC-D | Signed in as **Staff** (`lodu4289+staff@gmail.com`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| PC-E | Signed in as **Trainer** (`lodu4289+trainer@gmail.com`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| PC-F | Signed in as **Observer** (`lodu4289+observer@gmail.com`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| PC-G | **Ladyroom**: the ladyroom QA areas of the 029 plan §8 exist (created by E-02-G): `QA-E02-AREA-F` (JOYFIT, 女性のみ), `QA-E02-AREA-M` (JOYFIT, 男性のみ), `QA-E02-AREA-N` (JOYFIT, なし), `QA-E02-AREA-FIT` (FIT365, 女性のみ), plus the stress-name areas A-LJ / A-LT; the 029 §7e pre-flight (AREA-BE / AREA-MOCK) is done and the backend used is recorded. |

"Token captured" means the bearer token copied from any `api.dev…` request in DevTools for that account. "Replay" means re-sending the request with that token (DevTools "Copy as cURL" or curl). Never paste tokens into this document. "Read-back" means opening the saved master's detail `/contracts/[id]`, used as a tool only.

## Execution summary

All cases run at **1024px width or wider** (narrower than 1024px is out of scope). Viewport(s) used: **1024×768** (all layout / long-value cases and most others) and **1440×900** (wide check TC-126, detail read-backs). Executed 2026-09-29 against `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, app on `localhost:3000` (branch `feat/integration-api-plan`, working tree). Primary account Headquarter; System for TC-057/109/114.

**Run 2026-09-30**: TC-107, 134, 135, 136, 137 run against `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com` (app on `localhost:3000`, Headquarter account) at **1440×900**: 5 OK (TC-134 step 2 closed as a backend issue, not tracked - the backend reports `isCopyable:true` for the 特殊契約 fixture N7 / MC-0040, so the create-route guard for a special source only shows with the response intercepted; N6 / 子主契約 sources are guarded on the real backend). TC-136 forced `POST /admin/plans/<id>/images` to fail by route interception. TC-137 read the 税率 options: `10%`, `8%（軽減税率）`.

**Revision 2026-10-02**: TC-092 and TC-134 rewritten for the FR-M005 fix (複製 on a 子主契約 is enabled; 子主契約作成 stays blocked). Re-run the same day against `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com` (app on `localhost:3000`, Headquarter account, 1440×900): all OK. Fixture used: N4 MC-0038 / N6 MC-0057 / N7 MC-0040 (N7 with `isCopyable` intercepted to false — the backend still reports true for 特殊契約).

**Run 2026-10-02 (サムネイル type, QA Bug_No8)**: TC-020 revised and re-run, TC-138 added and run against `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com` (app on `localhost:3000`, Headquarter account, 1440×900): both OK after the BUG-G0101-15 fix. Nothing was saved: the two 1px PNGs uploaded to `plan-image` were never attached to a plan.
